Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the terrestrial framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 607. The resulting operator achieves bound O(427/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the terrestrial space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 607 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 427. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the terrestrial structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 851.
